{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01HqRAEHob7cGH5jtJgeyNX5","type":"tool_result","content":"607\t      # no managed auth, API key present) are reported, not fatal here: the\n608\t      # merge-time verify is the enforcement point and now fails with the\n609\t      # refusal's named cause instead of a bare \"attestation required\".\n610\t      - name: Probe and record runner attestation\n611\t        if: steps.lease_check.outputs.blocked != 'true' && steps.preflight.outputs.effective_mock == 'false'\n612\t        uses: ./.github/actions/probe-runner-attestation\n613\t        with:\n614\t          tools-dir: ${{ env.ARIA_TOOLS_DIR }}\n615\t\n616\t      # ORPHAN-HIGH-728 — WHICH PROFILE THE NIGHT RUNS UNDER IS A VERDICT\n617\t      # THE OPERATOR BOUNDS, NOT A LITERAL AND NOT A SELF-GRANT.\n618\t      #\n619\t      # This lane hardcoded `--profile standard` while `runtime_profile`\n620\t      # withholds `pr_create`/`pr_open` from standard, so every night ARIA\n621\t      # could converge a plan and then had no authority to open the pull\n622\t      # request that plan describes. The pipeline the profile table calls\n623\t      # \"full implementation (claim -> planned -> committed -> validated ->\n624\t      # PR)\" is `strict`, and this step is what asks whether the repository\n625\t      # has earned it tonight.\n626\t      #\n627\t      # Earning it is not the same as being granted it. ADR-033 records\n628\t      # strict as an operator decision and ADR-041 spends a week of observed\n629\t      # standard-profile nights before one; a lane that read its own\n630\t      # acceptance evidence as that grant would be handing itself the gesture\n631\t      # both documents reserve for a human — and would end the observation\n632\t      # window with the very event that window exists to produce. So the\n633\t      # answer is bounded by `scheduler_profile_ceiling`, an operator value\n634\t      # persisted through the EXISTING `runtime_profile` control plane\n635\t      # (default `standard`): the ladder chooses WITHIN the grant and can\n636\t      # never widen it. Raising it is one recorded command:\n637\t      #   aria-kernel profile set --profile standard \\\n638\t      #     --scheduler-ceiling strict --operator-approval-ref <ref>\n639\t      #\n640\t      # The decision itself lives in `autonomy_unlock.resolve_scheduled_profile`\n641\t      # and not in this heredoc, because it is a governed rule with three\n642\t      # inputs and a defined order; a copy of it in YAML would be the second\n643\t      # answer to \"may ARIA act\" that this whole change exists to delete. The\n644\t      # step prints what the kernel decided.\n645\t      #\n646\t      # The ladder is asked through `evaluate_autonomy_unlock`, the read-only\n647\t      # half: this step decides a profile, it does not spend the merge gate's\n648\t      # verdict row. An unreadable ladder DEMOTES to standard with a named\n649\t      # reason and does not fail the job — losing the night's discovery would\n650\t      # trade a missing PR for a missing cycle — and an operator hold\n651\t      # (`frozen`/`observe`) is returned before the ladder is read at all, so\n652\t      # a stopped kernel is not written to by the step deciding whether to\n653\t      # honour the stop.\n654\t      - name: Resolve the cycle profile within the operator ceiling\n655\t        id: profile_gate\n656\t        if: steps.lease_check.outputs.blocked != 'true' && (github.event.inputs.mode || 'cycle') == 'cycle'\n657\t        env:\n658\t          PYTHONPATH: aria-kernel\n659\t        run: |\n660\t          set -euo pipefail\n661\t          python3 - <<'PY'\n662\t          import os\n663\t          from pathlib import Path\n664\t\n665\t          from aria_kernel.autonomy_unlock import resolve_scheduled_profile\n666\t\n667\t          decision = resolve_scheduled_profile(\n668\t              lane=\"L1\", base_dir=Path(os.environ[\"ARIA_TOOLS_DIR\"]),\n669\t          )\n670\t          reasons = \"; \".join(decision.reasons)\n671\t          # `l1_valid` is three-valued on purpose: an operator hold means the\n672\t          # ladder was never asked, and \"false\" would misreport that as\n673\t          # evidence found wanting.\n674\t          l1_valid = (\n675\t              \"not-consulted\" if decision.l1_valid is None\n676\t              else str(decision.l1_valid).lower()\n677\t          )\n678\t          with open(os.environ[\"GITHUB_OUTPUT\"], \"a\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as handle:\n679\t              handle.write(f\"profile={decision.profile}\\n\")\n680\t              handle.write(f\"l1_valid={l1_valid}\\n\")\n681\t              handle.write(f\"ceiling={decision.ceiling}\\n\")\n682\t          # `reasons` deliberately does NOT become a step output: a reason\n683\t          # code carrying a newline would terminate the KEY=VALUE record and\n684\t          # let the rest be read as a further output. It reaches a human via\n685\t          # the summary and the annotation below, both of which are text.\n686\t          with open(os.environ[\"GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\"], \"a\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as handle:\n687\t              handle.write(\n688\t                  f\"### Cycle profile: `{decision.profile}` \"\n689\t                  f\"(L1 unlock valid={l1_valid})\\n\"\n690\t                  f\"- persisted profile on entry: `{decision.persisted}`\\n\"\n691\t                  f\"- operator scheduler ceiling: `{decision.ceiling}`\\n\"\n692\t                  f\"- ladder proposal: `{decision.ladder_profile}`\\n\"\n693\t                  f\"- counts: `{decision.counts}`\\n\"\n694\t                  f\"- reasons: {reasons}\\n\"\n695\t              )\n696\t          if decision.ladder_profile is None:\n697\t              print(\n698\t                  f\"::warning::operator_held_profile_preserved: \"\n699\t                  f\"{decision.persisted} holds no action authority; the \"\n700\t                  \"ladder was not consulted\"\n701\t              )\n702\t          elif decision.profile != decision.ladder_profile:\n703\t              print(\n704\t                  \"::warning::scheduler_ceiling_bound: the L1 ladder proposed \"\n705\t                  f\"{decision.ladder_profile}; the operator ceiling is \"\n706\t                  f\"{decision.ceiling}, so the cycle runs \"\n707\t                  f\"{decision.profile} ({reasons})\"\n708\t              )\n709\t          elif decision.l1_valid is not True:\n710\t              print(\n711\t                  \"::warning::l1_autonomy_unlock_invalid: refusing strict, \"\n712\t                  f\"running under {decision.profile} ({reasons})\"\n713\t              )\n714\t          PY\n715\t\n716\t      # ORPHAN-HIGH-728 — the SECOND preflight, and the one that can tell the\n717\t      # truth. The pre-restore preflight above runs before the state store is\n718\t      # bound, so it can only declare this lane's structural ceiling; by here\n719\t      # the profile is resolved, which is what makes\n720\t      # `frozen_profile_blocks_mutating_workflow` reachable on this lane at\n721\t      # all. That gate was dead code here while the declaration was a literal\n722\t      # — newly consequential now that the resolver deliberately PRESERVES a\n723\t      # frozen hold instead of thawing it: a kernel an operator stopped must\n724\t      # not have a workflow declaring write roots run against it.\n725\t      - name: Persist enterprise workflow preflight for the resolved profile\n726\t        if: steps.lease_check.outputs.blocked != 'true' && (github.event.inputs.mode || 'cycle') == 'cycle'\n727\t        env:\n728\t          PYTHONPATH: aria-kernel\n729\t          RESOLVED_PROFILE: ${{ steps.profile_gate.outputs.profile }}\n730\t        run: |\n731\t          set -euo pipefail\n732\t          python3 - <<'PY'\n733\t          import os\n734\t          from pathlib import Path\n735\t\n736\t          from aria_kernel.preflight import verify_workflow_preflight\n737\t\n738\t          allowed = [\".aria-state-store\"]\n739\t          verdict = verify_workflow_preflight(\n740\t              workflow_id=\"aria-auto-cycle\",\n741\t              job_id=\"cycle\",\n742\t              profile=os.environ[\"RESOLVED_PROFILE\"],\n743\t              workspace_root=os.environ[\"GITHUB_WORKSPACE\"],\n744\t              allowed_write_roots=allowed,\n745\t              path_allowlist=allowed,\n746\t              network_policy=[\"github_artifact\", \"github_git\"],\n747\t              network_enforcement_evidence=(\n748\t                  \"state restore/publish limited to the aria/state branch over \"\n749\t                  \"the repository remote; artifact upload limited to the \"\n750\t                  \"run-scoped aria-state-cache-<run_id> and \"\n751\t                  \"quarantine-evidence-<run_id> names, both via the Actions \"\n752\t                  \"artifact API\"\n753\t              ),\n754\t              token_provenance=\"github_actions_artifact_token\",\n755\t              require_github_app=False,\n756\t              dlp_mode=\"fail_closed\",\n757\t              dlp_scan_clean=True,\n758\t              audit_reason=(\n759\t                  \"nightly producer cycle over the durable aria/state store, \"\n760\t                  \"declared with the profile the run resolved within the \"\n761\t                  \"operator scheduler ceiling\"\n762\t              ),\n763\t              audit_artifact_path=(\n764\t                  Path(os.environ[\"RUNNER_TEMP\"])\n765\t                  / \"aria-auto-cycle-preflight-resolved.json\"\n766\t              ),\n767\t              external_root_allowlist=[str(Path(os.environ[\"RUNNER_TEMP\"]).resolve())],\n768\t          )\n769\t          if not verdict.valid:\n770\t              raise SystemExit(\";\".join(verdict.reasons))\n771\t          PY\n772\t\n773\t      - name: Run the nightly cycle under the resolved profile\n774\t        if: steps.lease_check.outputs.blocked != 'true' && (github.event.inputs.mode || 'cycle') == 'cycle'\n775\t        env:\n776\t          # ORPHAN-HIGH-626 — the pr_ci_scan phase's gh reads (own-PR list +\n777\t          # check verdicts) authenticate with the job token; without it the\n778\t          # checks reader reports gh_token_absent and the scan is a visible\n779\t          # no-op instead of a silent one.\n780\t          GH_TOKEN: ${{ github.token }}\n781\t          CLAUDE_CLI_MOCK: ${{ steps.preflight.outputs.effective_mock }}\n782\t          CLAUDE_CLI_MOCK_SOURCE: ${{ steps.preflight.outputs.mock_source }}\n783\t          ARIA_CLAUDE_REQUIRE_USAGE: '1'\n784\t          MAX_TURNS_PER_RUN: '12'\n785\t          # ARIA-HIGH-208 — the App a delivery mints its PR credential from.\n786\t          # ARIA_REQUIRE_MODE_A: that mint is Mode A or refuses by name; it\n787\t          # never copies the ambient GH_TOKEN (Mode B) into a PR's author.\n788\t          ARIA_REQUIRE_MODE_A: 'true'\n789\t          ARIA_GH_APP_ID: ${{ secrets.ARIA_GH_APP_ID }}\n790\t          ARIA_GH_APP_INSTALLATION_ID: ${{ secrets.ARIA_GH_APP_INSTALLATION_ID }}\n791\t          ARIA_GH_APP_PRIVATE_KEY: ${{ secrets.ARIA_GH_APP_PRIVATE_KEY }}\n792\t          MAX_REQUESTS_PER_RUN: '30'\n793\t          MAX_TIMEOUT_SECONDS: '1800'\n794\t          # Plan 032 Faz 032a — the dispatcher factories poll for an envelope\n795\t          # consumer INSIDE the cycle, and on this lane no consumer runs\n796\t          # until the executor lane starts after the cycle ends: every poll\n797\t          # structurally lost its full 600s (13/13 in production). Zero\n798\t          # returns the structured fallback at once; the executor still\n799\t          # drains the minted envelope, and the chain brings the answer back.\n800\t          ARIA_DISPATCHER_POLL_TIMEOUT_SECONDS: '0'\n801\t          MAX_CYCLES_INPUT: ${{ github.event.inputs.max_cycles }}\n802\t          # ORPHAN-HIGH-728 — the resolved verdict, carried as data. A\n803\t          # literal here is exactly the drift the gate above exists to\n804\t          # remove, and `autonomous` is unreachable from this lane by\n805\t          # construction: the resolver proposes at most\n806\t          # `SCHEDULER_MAX_PROPOSABLE_PROFILE` and then bounds that by the\n807\t          # operator ceiling.\n808\t          ARIA_CYCLE_PROFILE: ${{ steps.profile_gate.outputs.profile }}\n809\t          # `set_profile` demands an approval ref on EVERY transition and\n810\t          # synthesises `cli-flag:<daemon>:<epoch>` when the flag is absent\n811\t          # — a ref that names nothing an auditor can follow. The executor\n812\t          # lane enforces the PERSISTED profile, so this string is what a\n813\t          # later reader has to reconstruct the night's authority from: it\n814\t          # names the gate that granted it and the run that recorded it.\n815\t          ARIA_PROFILE_APPROVAL_REF: >-\n816\t            aria-auto-cycle:ceiling=${{ steps.profile_gate.outputs.ceiling }}:l1-unlock=${{ steps.profile_gate.outputs.l1_valid }}:run=${{ github.run_id }}\n817\t        run: |\n818\t          # ARIA-HIGH-208 (was ORPHAN-722) — PR identity. A PR opened with\n819\t          # the job token is authored by github-actions[bot] and GitHub parks\n820\t          # its workflows in action_required (anti-recursion, not a repo\n821\t          # setting): aria-merge-authority, the readiness claim and the merge\n822\t          # runner never start. Every PR this lane opens goes through a\n823\t          # delivery hold (`delivery_credentials`) that mints its own App\n824\t          # installation token where it is consumed and revokes it after;\n825\t          # ARIA_REQUIRE_MODE_A (env above) makes that mint refuse by name\n826\t          # when the App is not configured, instead of falling back.\n827\t          # ARIA-CRITICAL-246 — the ambient GH_TOKEN (env above) is the job\n828\t          # token and nothing else. It serves the lane's own gh reads and the\n829\t          # kernel's issue writes, which GitHub authors as a Bot. The runner\n830\t          # .env's operator PAT (ARIA_GH_TOKEN) is never exported: a write\n831\t          # made with it is authored by the operator, and an operator act is\n832\t          # what an ARIA-APPROVE line is verified against.\n833\t          set -euo pipefail\n834\t          # The App key, from the secret into a private file in RUNNER_TEMP:\n835\t          # outside the workspace every sandbox binds, removed on any exit.\n836\t          # The PEM leaves the environment before any child process starts;\n837\t          # the mint reads the file through ARIA_GH_APP_PRIVATE_KEY_PATH.\n838\t          KEY_FILE=\"$RUNNER_TEMP/aria-gh-app-private-key-${GITHUB_RUN_ID}-${GITHUB_RUN_ATTEMPT}.pem\"\n839\t          trap 'rm -f \"$KEY_FILE\"' EXIT\n840\t          (umask 077 && printf '%s\\n' \"$ARIA_GH_APP_PRIVATE_KEY\" > \"$KEY_FILE\")\n841\t          unset ARIA_GH_APP_PRIVATE_KEY\n842\t          export ARIA_GH_APP_PRIVATE_KEY_PATH=\"$KEY_FILE\"\n843\t          MAX_CYCLES=\"${MAX_CYCLES_INPUT:-1}\"\n844\t          if ! [[ \"$MAX_CYCLES\" =~ ^[0-9]{1,2}$ ]]; then\n845\t            echo \"::error::invalid max_cycles: ${MAX_CYCLES}\" >&2\n846\t            exit 1\n847\t          fi\n848\t          # Smoke-run 31645296013 — a spawn that cannot finish before the\n849\t          # job dies must not start. Export the ABSOLUTE deadline this job\n850\t          # runs under (its own timeout-minutes, minus 10 minutes for the\n851\t          # post-steps: handoff snapshot, state verify, publish) so\n852\t          # claude_runtime clamps every spawn's timeout to the remaining\n853\t          # wall-clock instead of letting the last spawn be cut mid-flight\n854\t          # and the whole night quarantine.\n855\t          # ARIA-HIGH-019 — the epoch is anchored at the job-launch step\n856\t          # near the top of this job ($ARIA_JOB_LAUNCH_EPOCH), NOT here: a\n857\t          # fresh-store restore measured 17m41s before this step (run\n858\t          # 32771477211), which consumed the margin computed from the step\n859\t          # clock and sealed the cycle 8 minutes past the platform ceiling.\n860\t          # Anchoring here is only the local-dispatch fallback (env unset).\n861\t          JOB_TIMEOUT_MINUTES=360\n862\t          ANCHOR_EPOCH=\"${ARIA_JOB_LAUNCH_EPOCH:-$(date +%s)}\"\n863\t          export ARIA_JOB_DEADLINE_EPOCH=$(( ANCHOR_EPOCH + JOB_TIMEOUT_MINUTES * 60 - 6000 ))\n864\t          echo \"spawn deadline: $(date -u -d @${ARIA_JOB_DEADLINE_EPOCH} +%H:%M:%SZ) (job cap ${JOB_TIMEOUT_MINUTES}m - 100m margin, anchored $( [ -n \"${ARIA_JOB_LAUNCH_EPOCH:-}\" ] && echo at-job-launch || echo at-step ))\"\n865\t          # The per-cycle wall clock is DERIVED from the job budget, not\n866\t          # hardcoded. It used to be a flat 1800s while the job cap was 360m:"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"8106c64f-84fe-4fc2-85a1-3e610198f667","uuid":"3e047ae5-14a4-43b4-be91-92e09ec4a1e8","timestamp":"2026-10-06T09:42:26.729Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-primary-planner-1ca79b2cdd2f/.github/workflows/aria-auto-cycle.yml","content":"      # no managed auth, API key present) are reported, not fatal here: the\n      # merge-time verify is the enforcement point and now fails with the\n      # refusal's named cause instead of a bare \"attestation required\".\n      - name: Probe and record runner attestation\n        if: steps.lease_check.outputs.blocked != 'true' && steps.preflight.outputs.effective_mock == 'false'\n        uses: ./.github/actions/probe-runner-attestation\n        with:\n          tools-dir: ${{ env.ARIA_TOOLS_DIR }}\n\n      # ORPHAN-HIGH-728 — WHICH PROFILE THE NIGHT RUNS UNDER IS A VERDICT\n      # THE OPERATOR BOUNDS, NOT A LITERAL AND NOT A SELF-GRANT.\n      #\n      # This lane hardcoded `--profile standard` while `runtime_profile`\n      # withholds `pr_create`/`pr_open` from standard, so every night ARIA\n      # could converge a plan and then had no authority to open the pull\n      # request that plan describes. The pipeline the profile table calls\n      # \"full implementation (claim -> planned -> committed -> validated ->\n      # PR)\" is `strict`, and this step is what asks whether the repository\n      # has earned it tonight.\n      #\n      # Earning it is not the same as being granted it. ADR-033 records\n      # strict as an operator decision and ADR-041 spends a week of observed\n      # standard-profile nights before one; a lane that read its own\n      # acceptance evidence as that grant would be handing itself the gesture\n      # both documents reserve for a human — and would end the observation\n      # window with the very event that window exists to produce. So the\n      # answer is bounded by `scheduler_profile_ceiling`, an operator value\n      # persisted through the EXISTING `runtime_profile` control plane\n      # (default `standard`): the ladder chooses WITHIN the grant and can\n      # never widen it. Raising it is one recorded command:\n      #   aria-kernel profile set --profile standard \\\n      #     --scheduler-ceiling strict --operator-approval-ref <ref>\n      #\n      # The decision itself lives in `autonomy_unlock.resolve_scheduled_profile`\n      # and not in this heredoc, because it is a governed rule with three\n      # inputs and a defined order; a copy of it in YAML would be the second\n      # answer to \"may ARIA act\" that this whole change exists to delete. The\n      # step prints what the kernel decided.\n      #\n      # The ladder is asked through `evaluate_autonomy_unlock`, the read-only\n      # half: this step decides a profile, it does not spend the merge gate's\n      # verdict row. An unreadable ladder DEMOTES to standard with a named\n      # reason and does not fail the job — losing the night's discovery would\n      # trade a missing PR for a missing cycle — and an operator hold\n      # (`frozen`/`observe`) is returned before the ladder is read at all, so\n      # a stopped kernel is not written to by the step deciding whether to\n      # honour the stop.\n      - name: Resolve the cycle profile within the operator ceiling\n        id: profile_gate\n        if: steps.lease_check.outputs.blocked != 'true' && (github.event.inputs.mode || 'cycle') == 'cycle'\n        env:\n          PYTHONPATH: aria-kernel\n        run: |\n          set -euo pipefail\n          python3 - <<'PY'\n          import os\n          from pathlib import Path\n\n          from aria_kernel.autonomy_unlock import resolve_scheduled_profile\n\n          decision = resolve_scheduled_profile(\n              lane=\"L1\", base_dir=Path(os.environ[\"ARIA_TOOLS_DIR\"]),\n          )\n          reasons = \"; \".join(decision.reasons)\n          # `l1_valid` is three-valued on purpose: an operator hold means the\n          # ladder was never asked, and \"false\" would misreport that as\n          # evidence found wanting.\n          l1_valid = (\n              \"not-consulted\" if decision.l1_valid is None\n              else str(decision.l1_valid).lower()\n          )\n          with open(os.environ[\"GITHUB_OUTPUT\"], \"a\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as handle:\n              handle.write(f\"profile={decision.profile}\\n\")\n              handle.write(f\"l1_valid={l1_valid}\\n\")\n              handle.write(f\"ceiling={decision.ceiling}\\n\")\n          # `reasons` deliberately does NOT become a step output: a reason\n          # code carrying a newline would terminate the KEY=VALUE record and\n          # let the rest be read as a further output. It reaches a human via\n          # the summary and the annotation below, both of which are text.\n          with open(os.environ[\"GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\"], \"a\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as handle:\n              handle.write(\n                  f\"### Cycle profile: `{decision.profile}` \"\n                  f\"(L1 unlock valid={l1_valid})\\n\"\n                  f\"- persisted profile on entry: `{decision.persisted}`\\n\"\n                  f\"- operator scheduler ceiling: `{decision.ceiling}`\\n\"\n                  f\"- ladder proposal: `{decision.ladder_profile}`\\n\"\n                  f\"- counts: `{decision.counts}`\\n\"\n                  f\"- reasons: {reasons}\\n\"\n              )\n          if decision.ladder_profile is None:\n              print(\n                  f\"::warning::operator_held_profile_preserved: \"\n                  f\"{decision.persisted} holds no action authority; the \"\n                  \"ladder was not consulted\"\n              )\n          elif decision.profile != decision.ladder_profile:\n              print(\n                  \"::warning::scheduler_ceiling_bound: the L1 ladder proposed \"\n                  f\"{decision.ladder_profile}; the operator ceiling is \"\n                  f\"{decision.ceiling}, so the cycle runs \"\n                  f\"{decision.profile} ({reasons})\"\n              )\n          elif decision.l1_valid is not True:\n              print(\n                  \"::warning::l1_autonomy_unlock_invalid: refusing strict, \"\n                  f\"running under {decision.profile} ({reasons})\"\n              )\n          PY\n\n      # ORPHAN-HIGH-728 — the SECOND preflight, and the one that can tell the\n      # truth. The pre-restore preflight above runs before the state store is\n      # bound, so it can only declare this lane's structural ceiling; by here\n      # the profile is resolved, which is what makes\n      # `frozen_profile_blocks_mutating_workflow` reachable on this lane at\n      # all. That gate was dead code here while the declaration was a literal\n      # — newly consequential now that the resolver deliberately PRESERVES a\n      # frozen hold instead of thawing it: a kernel an operator stopped must\n      # not have a workflow declaring write roots run against it.\n      - name: Persist enterprise workflow preflight for the resolved profile\n        if: steps.lease_check.outputs.blocked != 'true' && (github.event.inputs.mode || 'cycle') == 'cycle'\n        env:\n          PYTHONPATH: aria-kernel\n          RESOLVED_PROFILE: ${{ steps.profile_gate.outputs.profile }}\n        run: |\n          set -euo pipefail\n          python3 - <<'PY'\n          import os\n          from pathlib import Path\n\n          from aria_kernel.preflight import verify_workflow_preflight\n\n          allowed = [\".aria-state-store\"]\n          verdict = verify_workflow_preflight(\n              workflow_id=\"aria-auto-cycle\",\n              job_id=\"cycle\",\n              profile=os.environ[\"RESOLVED_PROFILE\"],\n              workspace_root=os.environ[\"GITHUB_WORKSPACE\"],\n              allowed_write_roots=allowed,\n              path_allowlist=allowed,\n              network_policy=[\"github_artifact\", \"github_git\"],\n              network_enforcement_evidence=(\n                  \"state restore/publish limited to the aria/state branch over \"\n                  \"the repository remote; artifact upload limited to the \"\n                  \"run-scoped aria-state-cache-<run_id> and \"\n                  \"quarantine-evidence-<run_id> names, both via the Actions \"\n                  \"artifact API\"\n              ),\n              token_provenance=\"github_actions_artifact_token\",\n              require_github_app=False,\n              dlp_mode=\"fail_closed\",\n              dlp_scan_clean=True,\n              audit_reason=(\n                  \"nightly producer cycle over the durable aria/state store, \"\n                  \"declared with the profile the run resolved within the \"\n                  \"operator scheduler ceiling\"\n              ),\n              audit_artifact_path=(\n                  Path(os.environ[\"RUNNER_TEMP\"])\n                  / \"aria-auto-cycle-preflight-resolved.json\"\n              ),\n              external_root_allowlist=[str(Path(os.environ[\"RUNNER_TEMP\"]).resolve())],\n          )\n          if not verdict.valid:\n              raise SystemExit(\";\".join(verdict.reasons))\n          PY\n\n      - name: Run the nightly cycle under the resolved profile\n        if: steps.lease_check.outputs.blocked != 'true' && (github.event.inputs.mode || 'cycle') == 'cycle'\n        env:\n          # ORPHAN-HIGH-626 — the pr_ci_scan phase's gh reads (own-PR list +\n          # check verdicts) authenticate with the job token; without it the\n          # checks reader reports gh_token_absent and the scan is a visible\n          # no-op instead of a silent one.\n          GH_TOKEN: ${{ github.token }}\n          CLAUDE_CLI_MOCK: ${{ steps.preflight.outputs.effective_mock }}\n          CLAUDE_CLI_MOCK_SOURCE: ${{ steps.preflight.outputs.mock_source }}\n          ARIA_CLAUDE_REQUIRE_USAGE: '1'\n          MAX_TURNS_PER_RUN: '12'\n          # ARIA-HIGH-208 — the App a delivery mints its PR credential from.\n          # ARIA_REQUIRE_MODE_A: that mint is Mode A or refuses by name; it\n          # never copies the ambient GH_TOKEN (Mode B) into a PR's author.\n          ARIA_REQUIRE_MODE_A: 'true'\n          ARIA_GH_APP_ID: ${{ secrets.ARIA_GH_APP_ID }}\n          ARIA_GH_APP_INSTALLATION_ID: ${{ secrets.ARIA_GH_APP_INSTALLATION_ID }}\n          ARIA_GH_APP_PRIVATE_KEY: ${{ secrets.ARIA_GH_APP_PRIVATE_KEY }}\n          MAX_REQUESTS_PER_RUN: '30'\n          MAX_TIMEOUT_SECONDS: '1800'\n          # Plan 032 Faz 032a — the dispatcher factories poll for an envelope\n          # consumer INSIDE the cycle, and on this lane no consumer runs\n          # until the executor lane starts after the cycle ends: every poll\n          # structurally lost its full 600s (13/13 in production). Zero\n          # returns the structured fallback at once; the executor still\n          # drains the minted envelope, and the chain brings the answer back.\n          ARIA_DISPATCHER_POLL_TIMEOUT_SECONDS: '0'\n          MAX_CYCLES_INPUT: ${{ github.event.inputs.max_cycles }}\n          # ORPHAN-HIGH-728 — the resolved verdict, carried as data. A\n          # literal here is exactly the drift the gate above exists to\n          # remove, and `autonomous` is unreachable from this lane by\n          # construction: the resolver proposes at most\n          # `SCHEDULER_MAX_PROPOSABLE_PROFILE` and then bounds that by the\n          # operator ceiling.\n          ARIA_CYCLE_PROFILE: ${{ steps.profile_gate.outputs.profile }}\n          # `set_profile` demands an approval ref on EVERY transition and\n          # synthesises `cli-flag:<daemon>:<epoch>` when the flag is absent\n          # — a ref that names nothing an auditor can follow. The executor\n          # lane enforces the PERSISTED profile, so this string is what a\n          # later reader has to reconstruct the night's authority from: it\n          # names the gate that granted it and the run that recorded it.\n          ARIA_PROFILE_APPROVAL_REF: >-\n            aria-auto-cycle:ceiling=${{ steps.profile_gate.outputs.ceiling }}:l1-unlock=${{ steps.profile_gate.outputs.l1_valid }}:run=${{ github.run_id }}\n        run: |\n          # ARIA-HIGH-208 (was ORPHAN-722) — PR identity. A PR opened with\n          # the job token is authored by github-actions[bot] and GitHub parks\n          # its workflows in action_required (anti-recursion, not a repo\n          # setting): aria-merge-authority, the readiness claim and the merge\n          # runner never start. Every PR this lane opens goes through a\n          # delivery hold (`delivery_credentials`) that mints its own App\n          # installation token where it is consumed and revokes it after;\n          # ARIA_REQUIRE_MODE_A (env above) makes that mint refuse by name\n          # when the App is not configured, instead of falling back.\n          # ARIA-CRITICAL-246 — the ambient GH_TOKEN (env above) is the job\n          # token and nothing else. It serves the lane's own gh reads and the\n          # kernel's issue writes, which GitHub authors as a Bot. The runner\n          # .env's operator PAT (ARIA_GH_TOKEN) is never exported: a write\n          # made with it is authored by the operator, and an operator act is\n          # what an ARIA-APPROVE line is verified against.\n          set -euo pipefail\n          # The App key, from the secret into a private file in RUNNER_TEMP:\n          # outside the workspace every sandbox binds, removed on any exit.\n          # The PEM leaves the environment before any child process starts;\n          # the mint reads the file through ARIA_GH_APP_PRIVATE_KEY_PATH.\n          KEY_FILE=\"$RUNNER_TEMP/aria-gh-app-private-key-${GITHUB_RUN_ID}-${GITHUB_RUN_ATTEMPT}.pem\"\n          trap 'rm -f \"$KEY_FILE\"' EXIT\n          (umask 077 && printf '%s\\n' \"$ARIA_GH_APP_PRIVATE_KEY\" > \"$KEY_FILE\")\n          unset ARIA_GH_APP_PRIVATE_KEY\n          export ARIA_GH_APP_PRIVATE_KEY_PATH=\"$KEY_FILE\"\n          MAX_CYCLES=\"${MAX_CYCLES_INPUT:-1}\"\n          if ! [[ \"$MAX_CYCLES\" =~ ^[0-9]{1,2}$ ]]; then\n            echo \"::error::invalid max_cycles: ${MAX_CYCLES}\" >&2\n            exit 1\n          fi\n          # Smoke-run 31645296013 — a spawn that cannot finish before the\n          # job dies must not start. Export the ABSOLUTE deadline this job\n          # runs under (its own timeout-minutes, minus 10 minutes for the\n          # post-steps: handoff snapshot, state verify, publish) so\n          # claude_runtime clamps every spawn's timeout to the remaining\n          # wall-clock instead of letting the last spawn be cut mid-flight\n          # and the whole night quarantine.\n          # ARIA-HIGH-019 — the epoch is anchored at the job-launch step\n          # near the top of this job ($ARIA_JOB_LAUNCH_EPOCH), NOT here: a\n          # fresh-store restore measured 17m41s before this step (run\n          # 32771477211), which consumed the margin computed from the step\n          # clock and sealed the cycle 8 minutes past the platform ceiling.\n          # Anchoring here is only the local-dispatch fallback (env unset).\n          JOB_TIMEOUT_MINUTES=360\n          ANCHOR_EPOCH=\"${ARIA_JOB_LAUNCH_EPOCH:-$(date +%s)}\"\n          export ARIA_JOB_DEADLINE_EPOCH=$(( ANCHOR_EPOCH + JOB_TIMEOUT_MINUTES * 60 - 6000 ))\n          echo \"spawn deadline: $(date -u -d @${ARIA_JOB_DEADLINE_EPOCH} +%H:%M:%SZ) (job cap ${JOB_TIMEOUT_MINUTES}m - 100m margin, anchored $( [ -n \"${ARIA_JOB_LAUNCH_EPOCH:-}\" ] && echo at-job-launch || echo at-step ))\"\n          # The per-cycle wall clock is DERIVED from the job budget, not\n          # hardcoded. It used to be a flat 1800s while the job cap was 360m:","numLines":260,"startLine":607,"totalLines":1149}}}
